FOE Calendar

Steps and code for the FOE app. Dummy data only: nothing is saved and nothing is connected to a list yet.

Update 25/11/2026: Step 2 (App.OnStart) now also builds the month list, and the Calendar, Admin, Home and Bars test files changed (month picker, colour dropdown, two formula fixes). Redo Steps 2, 3, 4, 5 and 8. If Studio shows "Pasting the YAML code for screen isn't supported yet", that is the co-authoring lock: Save, refresh the page, then paste again.
Stages 6 and 7 (24/11/2026): every file changed, so redo Steps 2 to 6 in order. For each screen: paste the new code over the old screen (or delete the old screen first) and keep the same screen name. The Calendar is now about 350 KB; if Studio refuses the paste, tell me and I will split the forms out.

1Create the app

  1. make.powerapps.com → Create → Blank canvas app. Name: FOE Calendar. Format: Tablet.
  2. Settings → Display: Landscape, 1366 x 768 or larger, Scale to fit on. Settings → Updates: modern controls on.
  3. No data connections yet.

2App.OnStart

Select App → OnStart, replace everything, paste. Then right-click App → Run OnStart.

ClearCollect(
    colEvents,
    ForAll(
        colBase As b,
        {
            Id: b.I,
            Title: b.T,
            StartDate: DateAdd(Today(), b.O, "Days"),
            EndDate: DateAdd(Today(), b.O + b.L - 1, "Days"),
            TypeKey: b.K,
            Status: If(b.S = "T", "To be confirmed", "Confirmed"),
            Kind: Coalesce(b.Kd, "event"),
            KeyDate: Coalesce(b.Kf, false),
            Overseas: Coalesce(b.Ov, false),
            Programme: Coalesce(b.Pg, "National Activity"),
            PeopleState: Coalesce(b.P, ""),
            PeopleNote: Coalesce(b.Pn, ""),
            MoneyState: Coalesce(b.M, ""),
            MoneyNote: Coalesce(b.Mn, ""),
            EquipState: Coalesce(b.E, ""),
            EquipNote: Coalesce(b.En, ""),
            BranchReq: Coalesce(b.B, false),
            BranchWho: Coalesce(b.W, ""),
            Location: "",
            Organiser: "",
            Notes: "",
            Sample: !IsBlank(b.P),
            Deleted: false,
            SortKey: b.O * 1000 - b.L
        }
    )
);
ClearCollect(colSelTypes, ForAll(Filter(colTypes, Active), {TKey: ThisRecord.TKey}));
ClearCollect(colScope, Filter(colEvents, !Deleted && (varProg = "all" || (varProg = "national" && Programme = "National Activity") || (varProg = "ctc" && Programme = "CTC") || (varProg = "ctt" && Programme = "CTTs") || (varProg = "overseas" && Overseas) || (varProg = "meet" && Kind <> "event"))));
ClearCollect(
    colVisible,
    AddColumns(
        SortByColumns(
            Filter(
                colScope,
                (TypeKey in colSelTypes.TKey) &&
                (varStatus = "All" || Status = varStatus) &&
                (IsBlank(varSearch) || Lower(varSearch) in Lower(Title))
            ),
            "SortKey",
            SortOrder.Ascending
        ),
        Concern,
        !Deleted && EndDate >= Today() && DateDiff(Today(), StartDate, "Days") <= varThresh && (PeopleState = "needs" || MoneyState = "needs" || EquipState = "needs"),
        Hex,
        With({k: TypeKey}, LookUp(colTypes, TKey = k).Hex)
    )
);
ClearCollect(
    colDays,
    ForAll(
        Sequence(42) As s,
        With(
            {d: DateAdd(varGridStart, s.Value - 1, "Days")},
            With(
                {f: Filter(colVisible, StartDate <= d && EndDate >= d)},
                With(
                    {e1: If(CountRows(f) >= 1, Index(f, 1)), e2: If(CountRows(f) >= 2, Index(f, 2)), e3: If(CountRows(f) >= 3, Index(f, 3))},
                    {
                        Idx: s.Value,
                        D: d,
                        H: Coalesce(LookUp(colBank, BD = d).Name, ""),
                        N: CountRows(f),
                        T1: If(IsBlank(e1), "", If(e1.Concern, "! ", "") & e1.Title),
                        F1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e1.Hex), 0.8)),
                        B1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e1.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e1.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                        W1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, If(e1.Concern, 2, If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                        S1: !IsBlank(e1) && e1.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e1.Concern,
                        I1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, e1.Id),
                        T2: If(IsBlank(e2), "", If(e2.Concern, "! ", "") & e2.Title),
                        F2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e2.Hex), 0.8)),
                        B2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e2.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e2.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                        W2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, If(e2.Concern, 2, If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                        S2: !IsBlank(e2) && e2.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e2.Concern,
                        I2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, e2.Id),
                        T3: If(IsBlank(e3), "", If(e3.Concern, "! ", "") & e3.Title),
                        F3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e3.Hex), 0.8)),
                        B3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e3.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e3.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                        W3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, If(e3.Concern, 2, If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                        S3: !IsBlank(e3) && e3.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e3.Concern,
                        I3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, e3.Id)
                    }
                )
            )
        )
    )
);
ClearCollect(colList, Filter(colVisible, false))

  scrCalendar:
    Properties:
      Fill: =RGBA(247, 244, 246, 1)

        Reset(drpStatus)

                  - btnTab_national:
                      Control: Classic/Button@2.2.0
                      Properties:
                        BorderThickness: =0
                        Color: =If(varProg = "national", RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1), RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1))
                        Fill: =If(varProg = "national", RGBA(247, 244, 246, 1), RGBA(255, 255, 255, 0.14))
                        FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                        Height: =34
                        HoverColor: =If(varProg = "national", RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1), RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1))
                        HoverFill: =If(varProg = "national", RGBA(247, 244, 246, 1), RGBA(255, 255, 255, 0.26))
                        OnSelect: |-
                          =Set(varProg, "national");
                          Set(varStatus, "All");
                          Set(varSearch, "");
                          Reset(txtSearch);
                          Reset(drpStatus);
                          ClearCollect(colSelTypes, ForAll(Filter(colTypes, Active), {TKey: ThisRecord.TKey}));
                          ClearCollect(colScope, Filter(colEvents, !Deleted && (varProg = "all" || (varProg = "national" && Programme = "National Activity") || (varProg = "ctc" && Programme = "CTC") || (varProg = "ctt" && Programme = "CTTs") || (varProg = "overseas" && Overseas) || (varProg = "meet" && Kind <> "event"))));
                          ClearCollect(
                              colVisible,
                              AddColumns(
                                  SortByColumns(
                                      Filter(
                                          colScope,
                                          (TypeKey in colSelTypes.TKey) &&
                                          (varStatus = "All" || Status = varStatus) &&
                                          (IsBlank(varSearch) || Lower(varSearch) in Lower(Title))
                                      ),
                                      "SortKey",
                                      SortOrder.Ascending
                                  ),
                                  Concern,
                                  !Deleted && EndDate >= Today() && DateDiff(Today(), StartDate, "Days") <= varThresh && (PeopleState = "needs" || MoneyState = "needs" || EquipState = "needs"),
                                  Hex,
                                  With({k: TypeKey}, LookUp(colTypes, TKey = k).Hex)
                              )
                          );
                          ClearCollect(
                              colDays,
                              ForAll(
                                  Sequence(42) As s,
                                  With(
                                      {d: DateAdd(varGridStart, s.Value - 1, "Days")},
                                      With(
                                          {f: Filter(colVisible, StartDate <= d && EndDate >= d)},
                                          With(
                                              {e1: If(CountRows(f) >= 1, Index(f, 1)), e2: If(CountRows(f) >= 2, Index(f, 2)), e3: If(CountRows(f) >= 3, Index(f, 3))},
                                              {
                                                  Idx: s.Value,
                                                  D: d,
                                                  H: Coalesce(LookUp(colBank, BD = d).Name, ""),
                                                  N: CountRows(f),
                                                  T1: If(IsBlank(e1), "", If(e1.Concern, "! ", "") & e1.Title),
                                                  F1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e1.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                  B1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e1.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e1.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                  W1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, If(e1.Concern, 2, If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                  S1: !IsBlank(e1) && e1.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e1.Concern,
                                                  I1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, e1.Id),
                                                  T2: If(IsBlank(e2), "", If(e2.Concern, "! ", "") & e2.Title),
                                                  F2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e2.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                  B2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e2.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e2.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                  W2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, If(e2.Concern, 2, If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                  S2: !IsBlank(e2) && e2.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e2.Concern,
                                                  I2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, e2.Id),
                                                  T3: If(IsBlank(e3), "", If(e3.Concern, "! ", "") & e3.Title),
                                                  F3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e3.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                  B3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e3.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e3.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                  W3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, If(e3.Concern, 2, If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                  S3: !IsBlank(e3) && e3.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e3.Concern,
                                                  I3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, e3.Id)
                                              }
                                          )
                                      )
                                  )
                              )
                          )
                        PressedColor: =If(varProg = "national", RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1), RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1))

                  - btnNSave:
                      Control: Classic/Button@2.2.0
                      Properties:
                        BorderThickness: =0
                        Color: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        Fill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                        Height: =34
                        HoverColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        HoverFill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        OnSelect: |-
                          =If(
                              IsBlank(Trim(txtNTitle.Text)),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Enter a title."),
                              IsBlank(dpkNStart.SelectedDate) || IsBlank(dpkNEnd.SelectedDate),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Choose a start date and an end date."),
                              dpkNEnd.SelectedDate < dpkNStart.SelectedDate,
                              Set(varFormErr, "The end date cannot be before the start date."),
                              IsBlank(drpNType.Selected),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Choose a type. If there is none, an admin can add one in the Admin area."),
                              tglNBranch.Value && IsBlank(Trim(txtNWho.Text)),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Branch attendance is required: say who is going, or untick the box."),
                              Set(varFormErr, "");
                              If(
                                  varFormMode = "edit",
                                  Patch(
                                      colEvents,
                                      LookUp(colEvents, Id = varEvent.Id),
                                      {
                                      Title: Trim(txtNTitle.Text),
                                      StartDate: dpkNStart.SelectedDate,
                                      EndDate: dpkNEnd.SelectedDate,
                                      TypeKey: drpNType.Selected.TKey,
                                      Status: drpNStatus.Selected.Value,
                                      Programme: "National Activity",
                                      Kind: "event",
                                      KeyDate: tglNKey.Value,
                                      BranchReq: tglNBranch.Value,
                                      BranchWho: Trim(txtNWho.Text),
                                      PeopleState: drpNResP.Selected.Code,
                                      PeopleNote: Trim(txtNResP.Text),
                                      MoneyState: drpNResM.Selected.Code,
                                      MoneyNote: Trim(txtNResM.Text),
                                      EquipState: drpNResE.Selected.Code,
                                      EquipNote: Trim(txtNResE.Text),
                                      Location: Trim(txtNLoc.Text),
                                      Organiser: Trim(txtNOrg.Text),
                                      Notes: Trim(txtNNotes.Text),
                                      SortKey: DateDiff(Today(), dpkNStart.SelectedDate, "Days") * 1000 - (DateDiff(dpkNStart.SelectedDate, dpkNEnd.SelectedDate, "Days") + 1)
                                      }
                                  );
                                  Set(varEvent, LookUp(colEvents, Id = varEvent.Id)),
                                  Set(varNewId, Coalesce(Max(colEvents, Id), 0) + 1);
                                  Collect(
                                      colEvents,
                                      {
                                      Id: varNewId,
                                      Sample: false,
                                      Deleted: false,
                                      Overseas: false,
                                      Title: Trim(txtNTitle.Text),
                                      StartDate: dpkNStart.SelectedDate,
                                      EndDate: dpkNEnd.SelectedDate,
                                      TypeKey: drpNType.Selected.TKey,
                                      Status: drpNStatus.Selected.Value,
                                      Programme: "National Activity",
                                      Kind: "event",
                                      KeyDate: tglNKey.Value,
                                      BranchReq: tglNBranch.Value,
                                      BranchWho: Trim(txtNWho.Text),
                                      PeopleState: drpNResP.Selected.Code,
                                      PeopleNote: Trim(txtNResP.Text),
                                      MoneyState: drpNResM.Selected.Code,
                                      MoneyNote: Trim(txtNResM.Text),
                                      EquipState: drpNResE.Selected.Code,
                                      EquipNote: Trim(txtNResE.Text),
                                      Location: Trim(txtNLoc.Text),
                                      Organiser: Trim(txtNOrg.Text),
                                      Notes: Trim(txtNNotes.Text),
                                      SortKey: DateDiff(Today(), dpkNStart.SelectedDate, "Days") * 1000 - (DateDiff(dpkNStart.SelectedDate, dpkNEnd.SelectedDate, "Days") + 1)
                                      }
                                  )
                              );
                              ClearCollect(colScope, Filter(colEvents, !Deleted && (varProg = "all" || (varProg = "national" && Programme = "National Activity") || (varProg = "ctc" && Programme = "CTC") || (varProg = "ctt" && Programme = "CTTs") || (varProg = "overseas" && Overseas) || (varProg = "meet" && Kind <> "event"))));
                              ClearCollect(
                                  colVisible,
                                  AddColumns(
                                      SortByColumns(
                                          Filter(
                                              colScope,
                                              (TypeKey in colSelTypes.TKey) &&
                                              (varStatus = "All" || Status = varStatus) &&
                                              (IsBlank(varSearch) || Lower(varSearch) in Lower(Title))
                                          ),
                                          "SortKey",
                                          SortOrder.Ascending
                                      ),
                                      Concern,
                                      !Deleted && EndDate >= Today() && DateDiff(Today(), StartDate, "Days") <= varThresh && (PeopleState = "needs" || MoneyState = "needs" || EquipState = "needs"),
                                      Hex,
                                      With({k: TypeKey}, LookUp(colTypes, TKey = k).Hex)
                                  )
                              );
                              ClearCollect(
                                  colDays,
                                  ForAll(
                                      Sequence(42) As s,
                                      With(
                                          {d: DateAdd(varGridStart, s.Value - 1, "Days")},
                                          With(
                                              {f: Filter(colVisible, StartDate <= d && EndDate >= d)},
                                              With(
                                                  {e1: If(CountRows(f) >= 1, Index(f, 1)), e2: If(CountRows(f) >= 2, Index(f, 2)), e3: If(CountRows(f) >= 3, Index(f, 3))},
                                                  {
                                                      Idx: s.Value,
                                                      D: d,
                                                      H: Coalesce(LookUp(colBank, BD = d).Name, ""),
                                                      N: CountRows(f),
                                                      T1: If(IsBlank(e1), "", If(e1.Concern, "! ", "") & e1.Title),
                                                      F1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e1.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e1.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e1.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, If(e1.Concern, 2, If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S1: !IsBlank(e1) && e1.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e1.Concern,
                                                      I1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, e1.Id),
                                                      T2: If(IsBlank(e2), "", If(e2.Concern, "! ", "") & e2.Title),
                                                      F2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e2.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e2.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e2.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, If(e2.Concern, 2, If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S2: !IsBlank(e2) && e2.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e2.Concern,
                                                      I2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, e2.Id),
                                                      T3: If(IsBlank(e3), "", If(e3.Concern, "! ", "") & e3.Title),
                                                      F3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e3.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e3.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e3.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, If(e3.Concern, 2, If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S3: !IsBlank(e3) && e3.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e3.Concern,
                                                      I3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, e3.Id)
                                                  }
                                              )
                                          )
                                      )
                                  )
                              );
                              Notify(If(varFormMode = "edit", "Changes saved", "Added") & " (dummy data only: not stored).", NotificationType.Success);
                              Set(varDlg, If(varFormMode = "edit", "detail", ""))
                          )
                        PressedColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        PressedFill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        RadiusBottomLeft: =17
                        RadiusBottomRight: =17
                        RadiusTopLeft: =17
                        RadiusTopRight: =17
                        Size: =11
                        Text: =If(varFormMode = "edit", "Save changes", "Save")
                        Width: =120

                        PressedColor: =RGBA(30, 24, 34, 1)

                  - btnOSave:
                      Control: Classic/Button@2.2.0
                      Properties:
                        BorderThickness: =0
                        Color: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        Fill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                        Height: =34
                        HoverColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        HoverFill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        OnSelect: |-
                          =If(
                              IsBlank(Trim(txtOTitle.Text)),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Enter a title."),
                              IsBlank(dpkOStart.SelectedDate) || IsBlank(dpkOEnd.SelectedDate),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Choose a start date and an end date."),
                              dpkOEnd.SelectedDate < dpkOStart.SelectedDate,
                              Set(varFormErr, "The end date cannot be before the start date."),
                              IsBlank(drpOType.Selected),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Choose a type. If there is none, an admin can add one in the Admin area."),
                              tglOBranch.Value && IsBlank(Trim(txtOWho.Text)),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Branch attendance is required: say who is going, or untick the box."),
                              Set(varFormErr, "");
                              If(
                                  varFormMode = "edit",
                                  Patch(
                                      colEvents,
                                      LookUp(colEvents, Id = varEvent.Id),
                                      {
                                      Title: Trim(txtOTitle.Text),
                                      StartDate: dpkOStart.SelectedDate,
                                      EndDate: dpkOEnd.SelectedDate,
                                      TypeKey: drpOType.Selected.TKey,
                                      Status: drpOStatus.Selected.Value,
                                      Programme: drpOProg.Selected.Value,
                                      Kind: "event",
                                      KeyDate: tglOKey.Value,
                                      Overseas: true,
                                      BranchReq: tglOBranch.Value,
                                      BranchWho: Trim(txtOWho.Text),
                                      PeopleState: drpOResP.Selected.Code,
                                      PeopleNote: Trim(txtOResP.Text),
                                      MoneyState: drpOResM.Selected.Code,
                                      MoneyNote: Trim(txtOResM.Text),
                                      EquipState: drpOResE.Selected.Code,
                                      EquipNote: Trim(txtOResE.Text),
                                      Location: Trim(txtOLoc.Text),
                                      Organiser: Trim(txtOOrg.Text),
                                      Notes: Trim(txtONotes.Text),
                                      SortKey: DateDiff(Today(), dpkOStart.SelectedDate, "Days") * 1000 - (DateDiff(dpkOStart.SelectedDate, dpkOEnd.SelectedDate, "Days") + 1)
                                      }
                                  );
                                  Set(varEvent, LookUp(colEvents, Id = varEvent.Id)),
                                  Set(varNewId, Coalesce(Max(colEvents, Id), 0) + 1);
                                  Collect(
                                      colEvents,
                                      {
                                      Id: varNewId,
                                      Sample: false,
                                      Deleted: false,
                                      Title: Trim(txtOTitle.Text),
                                      StartDate: dpkOStart.SelectedDate,
                                      EndDate: dpkOEnd.SelectedDate,
                                      TypeKey: drpOType.Selected.TKey,
                                      Status: drpOStatus.Selected.Value,
                                      Programme: drpOProg.Selected.Value,
                                      Kind: "event",
                                      KeyDate: tglOKey.Value,
                                      Overseas: true,
                                      BranchReq: tglOBranch.Value,
                                      BranchWho: Trim(txtOWho.Text),
                                      PeopleState: drpOResP.Selected.Code,
                                      PeopleNote: Trim(txtOResP.Text),
                                      MoneyState: drpOResM.Selected.Code,
                                      MoneyNote: Trim(txtOResM.Text),
                                      EquipState: drpOResE.Selected.Code,
                                      EquipNote: Trim(txtOResE.Text),
                                      Location: Trim(txtOLoc.Text),
                                      Organiser: Trim(txtOOrg.Text),
                                      Notes: Trim(txtONotes.Text),
                                      SortKey: DateDiff(Today(), dpkOStart.SelectedDate, "Days") * 1000 - (DateDiff(dpkOStart.SelectedDate, dpkOEnd.SelectedDate, "Days") + 1)
                                      }
                                  )
                              );
                              ClearCollect(colScope, Filter(colEvents, !Deleted && (varProg = "all" || (varProg = "national" && Programme = "National Activity") || (varProg = "ctc" && Programme = "CTC") || (varProg = "ctt" && Programme = "CTTs") || (varProg = "overseas" && Overseas) || (varProg = "meet" && Kind <> "event"))));
                              ClearCollect(
                                  colVisible,
                                  AddColumns(
                                      SortByColumns(
                                          Filter(
                                              colScope,
                                              (TypeKey in colSelTypes.TKey) &&
                                              (varStatus = "All" || Status = varStatus) &&
                                              (IsBlank(varSearch) || Lower(varSearch) in Lower(Title))
                                          ),
                                          "SortKey",
                                          SortOrder.Ascending
                                      ),
                                      Concern,
                                      !Deleted && EndDate >= Today() && DateDiff(Today(), StartDate, "Days") <= varThresh && (PeopleState = "needs" || MoneyState = "needs" || EquipState = "needs"),
                                      Hex,
                                      With({k: TypeKey}, LookUp(colTypes, TKey = k).Hex)
                                  )
                              );
                              ClearCollect(
                                  colDays,
                                  ForAll(
                                      Sequence(42) As s,
                                      With(
                                          {d: DateAdd(varGridStart, s.Value - 1, "Days")},
                                          With(
                                              {f: Filter(colVisible, StartDate <= d && EndDate >= d)},
                                              With(
                                                  {e1: If(CountRows(f) >= 1, Index(f, 1)), e2: If(CountRows(f) >= 2, Index(f, 2)), e3: If(CountRows(f) >= 3, Index(f, 3))},
                                                  {
                                                      Idx: s.Value,
                                                      D: d,
                                                      H: Coalesce(LookUp(colBank, BD = d).Name, ""),
                                                      N: CountRows(f),
                                                      T1: If(IsBlank(e1), "", If(e1.Concern, "! ", "") & e1.Title),
                                                      F1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e1.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e1.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e1.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, If(e1.Concern, 2, If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S1: !IsBlank(e1) && e1.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e1.Concern,
                                                      I1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, e1.Id),
                                                      T2: If(IsBlank(e2), "", If(e2.Concern, "! ", "") & e2.Title),
                                                      F2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e2.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e2.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e2.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, If(e2.Concern, 2, If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S2: !IsBlank(e2) && e2.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e2.Concern,
                                                      I2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, e2.Id),
                                                      T3: If(IsBlank(e3), "", If(e3.Concern, "! ", "") & e3.Title),
                                                      F3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e3.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e3.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e3.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, If(e3.Concern, 2, If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S3: !IsBlank(e3) && e3.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e3.Concern,
                                                      I3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, e3.Id)
                                                  }
                                              )
                                          )
                                      )
                                  )
                              );
                              Notify(If(varFormMode = "edit", "Changes saved", "Added") & " (dummy data only: not stored).", NotificationType.Success);
                              Set(varDlg, If(varFormMode = "edit", "detail", ""))
                          )
                        PressedColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        PressedFill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        RadiusBottomLeft: =17
                        RadiusBottomRight: =17
                        RadiusTopLeft: =17
                        RadiusTopRight: =17
                        Size: =11
                        Text: =If(varFormMode = "edit", "Save changes", "Save")
                        Width: =120
                        X: =Parent.Width - 136

                  - btnCSave:
                      Control: Classic/Button@2.2.0
                      Properties:
                        BorderThickness: =0
                        Color: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        Fill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                        Height: =34
                        HoverColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        HoverFill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        OnSelect: |-
                          =If(
                              IsBlank(Trim(txtCTitle.Text)),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Enter a title."),
                              IsBlank(dpkCStart.SelectedDate) || IsBlank(dpkCEnd.SelectedDate),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Choose a start date and an end date."),
                              dpkCEnd.SelectedDate < dpkCStart.SelectedDate,
                              Set(varFormErr, "The end date cannot be before the start date."),
                              IsBlank(drpCType.Selected),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Choose a type. If there is none, an admin can add one in the Admin area."),
                              tglCBranch.Value && IsBlank(Trim(txtCWho.Text)),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Branch attendance is required: say who is going, or untick the box."),
                              Set(varFormErr, "");
                              If(
                                  varFormMode = "edit",
                                  Patch(
                                      colEvents,
                                      LookUp(colEvents, Id = varEvent.Id),
                                      {
                                      Title: Trim(txtCTitle.Text),
                                      StartDate: dpkCStart.SelectedDate,
                                      EndDate: dpkCEnd.SelectedDate,
                                      TypeKey: drpCType.Selected.TKey,
                                      Status: drpCStatus.Selected.Value,
                                      Programme: "CTC",
                                      Kind: "event",
                                      KeyDate: tglCKey.Value,
                                      BranchReq: tglCBranch.Value,
                                      BranchWho: Trim(txtCWho.Text),
                                      PeopleState: drpCResP.Selected.Code,
                                      PeopleNote: Trim(txtCResP.Text),
                                      MoneyState: drpCResM.Selected.Code,
                                      MoneyNote: Trim(txtCResM.Text),
                                      EquipState: drpCResE.Selected.Code,
                                      EquipNote: Trim(txtCResE.Text),
                                      Location: Trim(txtCLoc.Text),
                                      Organiser: Trim(txtCOrg.Text),
                                      Notes: Trim(txtCNotes.Text),
                                      SortKey: DateDiff(Today(), dpkCStart.SelectedDate, "Days") * 1000 - (DateDiff(dpkCStart.SelectedDate, dpkCEnd.SelectedDate, "Days") + 1)
                                      }
                                  );
                                  Set(varEvent, LookUp(colEvents, Id = varEvent.Id)),
                                  Set(varNewId, Coalesce(Max(colEvents, Id), 0) + 1);
                                  Collect(
                                      colEvents,
                                      {
                                      Id: varNewId,
                                      Sample: false,
                                      Deleted: false,
                                      Overseas: false,
                                      Title: Trim(txtCTitle.Text),
                                      StartDate: dpkCStart.SelectedDate,
                                      EndDate: dpkCEnd.SelectedDate,
                                      TypeKey: drpCType.Selected.TKey,
                                      Status: drpCStatus.Selected.Value,
                                      Programme: "CTC",
                                      Kind: "event",
                                      KeyDate: tglCKey.Value,
                                      BranchReq: tglCBranch.Value,
                                      BranchWho: Trim(txtCWho.Text),
                                      PeopleState: drpCResP.Selected.Code,
                                      PeopleNote: Trim(txtCResP.Text),
                                      MoneyState: drpCResM.Selected.Code,
                                      MoneyNote: Trim(txtCResM.Text),
                                      EquipState: drpCResE.Selected.Code,
                                      EquipNote: Trim(txtCResE.Text),
                                      Location: Trim(txtCLoc.Text),
                                      Organiser: Trim(txtCOrg.Text),
                                      Notes: Trim(txtCNotes.Text),
                                      SortKey: DateDiff(Today(), dpkCStart.SelectedDate, "Days") * 1000 - (DateDiff(dpkCStart.SelectedDate, dpkCEnd.SelectedDate, "Days") + 1)
                                      }
                                  )
                              );
                              ClearCollect(colScope, Filter(colEvents, !Deleted && (varProg = "all" || (varProg = "national" && Programme = "National Activity") || (varProg = "ctc" && Programme = "CTC") || (varProg = "ctt" && Programme = "CTTs") || (varProg = "overseas" && Overseas) || (varProg = "meet" && Kind <> "event"))));
                              ClearCollect(
                                  colVisible,
                                  AddColumns(
                                      SortByColumns(
                                          Filter(
                                              colScope,
                                              (TypeKey in colSelTypes.TKey) &&
                                              (varStatus = "All" || Status = varStatus) &&
                                              (IsBlank(varSearch) || Lower(varSearch) in Lower(Title))
                                          ),
                                          "SortKey",
                                          SortOrder.Ascending
                                      ),
                                      Concern,
                                      !Deleted && EndDate >= Today() && DateDiff(Today(), StartDate, "Days") <= varThresh && (PeopleState = "needs" || MoneyState = "needs" || EquipState = "needs"),
                                      Hex,
                                      With({k: TypeKey}, LookUp(colTypes, TKey = k).Hex)
                                  )
                              );
                              ClearCollect(
                                  colDays,
                                  ForAll(
                                      Sequence(42) As s,
                                      With(
                                          {d: DateAdd(varGridStart, s.Value - 1, "Days")},
                                          With(
                                              {f: Filter(colVisible, StartDate <= d && EndDate >= d)},
                                              With(
                                                  {e1: If(CountRows(f) >= 1, Index(f, 1)), e2: If(CountRows(f) >= 2, Index(f, 2)), e3: If(CountRows(f) >= 3, Index(f, 3))},
                                                  {
                                                      Idx: s.Value,
                                                      D: d,
                                                      H: Coalesce(LookUp(colBank, BD = d).Name, ""),
                                                      N: CountRows(f),
                                                      T1: If(IsBlank(e1), "", If(e1.Concern, "! ", "") & e1.Title),
                                                      F1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e1.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e1.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e1.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, If(e1.Concern, 2, If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S1: !IsBlank(e1) && e1.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e1.Concern,
                                                      I1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, e1.Id),
                                                      T2: If(IsBlank(e2), "", If(e2.Concern, "! ", "") & e2.Title),
                                                      F2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e2.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e2.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e2.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, If(e2.Concern, 2, If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S2: !IsBlank(e2) && e2.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e2.Concern,
                                                      I2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, e2.Id),
                                                      T3: If(IsBlank(e3), "", If(e3.Concern, "! ", "") & e3.Title),
                                                      F3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e3.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e3.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e3.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, If(e3.Concern, 2, If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S3: !IsBlank(e3) && e3.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e3.Concern,
                                                      I3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, e3.Id)
                                                  }
                                              )
                                          )
                                      )
                                  )
                              );
                              Notify(If(varFormMode = "edit", "Changes saved", "Added") & " (dummy data only: not stored).", NotificationType.Success);
                              Set(varDlg, If(varFormMode = "edit", "detail", ""))
                          )
                        PressedColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        PressedFill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        RadiusBottomLeft: =17
                        RadiusBottomRight: =17
                        RadiusTopLeft: =17
                        RadiusTopRight: =17
                        Size: =11
                        Text: =If(varFormMode = "edit", "Save changes", "Save")
                        Width: =120

                  - btnTSave:
                      Control: Classic/Button@2.2.0
                      Properties:
                        BorderThickness: =0
                        Color: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        Fill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                        Height: =34
                        HoverColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        HoverFill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        OnSelect: |-
                          =If(
                              IsBlank(Trim(txtTTitle.Text)),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Enter a title."),
                              IsBlank(dpkTStart.SelectedDate) || IsBlank(dpkTEnd.SelectedDate),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Choose a start date and an end date."),
                              dpkTEnd.SelectedDate < dpkTStart.SelectedDate,
                              Set(varFormErr, "The end date cannot be before the start date."),
                              IsBlank(drpTType.Selected),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Choose a type. If there is none, an admin can add one in the Admin area."),
                              Set(varFormErr, "");
                              If(
                                  varFormMode = "edit",
                                  Patch(
                                      colEvents,
                                      LookUp(colEvents, Id = varEvent.Id),
                                      {
                                      Title: Trim(txtTTitle.Text),
                                      StartDate: dpkTStart.SelectedDate,
                                      EndDate: dpkTEnd.SelectedDate,
                                      TypeKey: drpTType.Selected.TKey,
                                      Status: drpTStatus.Selected.Value,
                                      Programme: "CTTs",
                                      Kind: "event",
                                      KeyDate: tglTKey.Value,
                                      PeopleState: drpTResP.Selected.Code,
                                      PeopleNote: Trim(txtTResP.Text),
                                      MoneyState: drpTResM.Selected.Code,
                                      MoneyNote: Trim(txtTResM.Text),
                                      EquipState: drpTResE.Selected.Code,
                                      EquipNote: Trim(txtTResE.Text),
                                      Location: Trim(txtTLoc.Text),
                                      Organiser: Trim(txtTOrg.Text),
                                      Notes: Trim(txtTNotes.Text),
                                      SortKey: DateDiff(Today(), dpkTStart.SelectedDate, "Days") * 1000 - (DateDiff(dpkTStart.SelectedDate, dpkTEnd.SelectedDate, "Days") + 1)
                                      }
                                  );
                                  Set(varEvent, LookUp(colEvents, Id = varEvent.Id)),
                                  Set(varNewId, Coalesce(Max(colEvents, Id), 0) + 1);
                                  Collect(
                                      colEvents,
                                      {
                                      Id: varNewId,
                                      Sample: false,
                                      Deleted: false,
                                      Overseas: false,
                                      BranchReq: false,
                                      BranchWho: "",
                                      Title: Trim(txtTTitle.Text),
                                      StartDate: dpkTStart.SelectedDate,
                                      EndDate: dpkTEnd.SelectedDate,
                                      TypeKey: drpTType.Selected.TKey,
                                      Status: drpTStatus.Selected.Value,
                                      Programme: "CTTs",
                                      Kind: "event",
                                      KeyDate: tglTKey.Value,
                                      PeopleState: drpTResP.Selected.Code,
                                      PeopleNote: Trim(txtTResP.Text),
                                      MoneyState: drpTResM.Selected.Code,
                                      MoneyNote: Trim(txtTResM.Text),
                                      EquipState: drpTResE.Selected.Code,
                                      EquipNote: Trim(txtTResE.Text),
                                      Location: Trim(txtTLoc.Text),
                                      Organiser: Trim(txtTOrg.Text),
                                      Notes: Trim(txtTNotes.Text),
                                      SortKey: DateDiff(Today(), dpkTStart.SelectedDate, "Days") * 1000 - (DateDiff(dpkTStart.SelectedDate, dpkTEnd.SelectedDate, "Days") + 1)
                                      }
                                  )
                              );
                              ClearCollect(colScope, Filter(colEvents, !Deleted && (varProg = "all" || (varProg = "national" && Programme = "National Activity") || (varProg = "ctc" && Programme = "CTC") || (varProg = "ctt" && Programme = "CTTs") || (varProg = "overseas" && Overseas) || (varProg = "meet" && Kind <> "event"))));
                              ClearCollect(
                                  colVisible,
                                  AddColumns(
                                      SortByColumns(
                                          Filter(
                                              colScope,
                                              (TypeKey in colSelTypes.TKey) &&
                                              (varStatus = "All" || Status = varStatus) &&
                                              (IsBlank(varSearch) || Lower(varSearch) in Lower(Title))
                                          ),
                                          "SortKey",
                                          SortOrder.Ascending
                                      ),
                                      Concern,
                                      !Deleted && EndDate >= Today() && DateDiff(Today(), StartDate, "Days") <= varThresh && (PeopleState = "needs" || MoneyState = "needs" || EquipState = "needs"),
                                      Hex,
                                      With({k: TypeKey}, LookUp(colTypes, TKey = k).Hex)
                                  )
                              );
                              ClearCollect(
                                  colDays,
                                  ForAll(
                                      Sequence(42) As s,
                                      With(
                                          {d: DateAdd(varGridStart, s.Value - 1, "Days")},
                                          With(
                                              {f: Filter(colVisible, StartDate <= d && EndDate >= d)},
                                              With(
                                                  {e1: If(CountRows(f) >= 1, Index(f, 1)), e2: If(CountRows(f) >= 2, Index(f, 2)), e3: If(CountRows(f) >= 3, Index(f, 3))},
                                                  {
                                                      Idx: s.Value,
                                                      D: d,
                                                      H: Coalesce(LookUp(colBank, BD = d).Name, ""),
                                                      N: CountRows(f),
                                                      T1: If(IsBlank(e1), "", If(e1.Concern, "! ", "") & e1.Title),
                                                      F1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e1.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e1.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e1.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, If(e1.Concern, 2, If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S1: !IsBlank(e1) && e1.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e1.Concern,
                                                      I1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, e1.Id),
                                                      T2: If(IsBlank(e2), "", If(e2.Concern, "! ", "") & e2.Title),
                                                      F2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e2.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e2.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e2.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, If(e2.Concern, 2, If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S2: !IsBlank(e2) && e2.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e2.Concern,
                                                      I2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, e2.Id),
                                                      T3: If(IsBlank(e3), "", If(e3.Concern, "! ", "") & e3.Title),
                                                      F3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e3.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e3.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e3.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, If(e3.Concern, 2, If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S3: !IsBlank(e3) && e3.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e3.Concern,
                                                      I3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, e3.Id)
                                                  }
                                              )
                                          )
                                      )
                                  )
                              );
                              Notify(If(varFormMode = "edit", "Changes saved", "Added") & " (dummy data only: not stored).", NotificationType.Success);
                              Set(varDlg, If(varFormMode = "edit", "detail", ""))
                          )
                        PressedColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        PressedFill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        RadiusBottomLeft: =17
                        RadiusBottomRight: =17
                        RadiusTopLeft: =17
                        RadiusTopRight: =17

                        HoverFill: =RGBA(240, 236, 240, 1)
                        OnSelect: =Set(varDlg, If(varFormMode = "edit", "detail", ""))
                        PressedColor: =RGBA(30, 24, 34, 1)
                        PressedFill: =RGBA(240, 236, 240, 1)
                        RadiusBottomLeft: =17
                        RadiusBottomRight: =17
                        RadiusTopLeft: =17
                        RadiusTopRight: =17
                        Size: =11
                        Text: ="Cancel"
                        Width: =110
                        X: =Parent.Width - 252

                  - btnMSave:
                      Control: Classic/Button@2.2.0
                      Properties:
                        BorderThickness: =0
                        Color: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        Fill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                        Height: =34
                        HoverColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        HoverFill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        OnSelect: |-
                          =If(
                              IsBlank(Trim(txtMTitle.Text)),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Enter a title."),
                              IsBlank(dpkMStart.SelectedDate) || IsBlank(dpkMEnd.SelectedDate),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Choose a start date and an end date."),
                              dpkMEnd.SelectedDate < dpkMStart.SelectedDate,
                              Set(varFormErr, "The end date cannot be before the start date."),
                              IsBlank(drpMType.Selected),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Choose a type. If there is none, an admin can add one in the Admin area."),
                              tglMBranch.Value && IsBlank(Trim(txtMWho.Text)),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Branch attendance is required: say who is going, or untick the box."),
                              varFormMode <> "edit" && drpMRepeat.Selected.Value <> "none" && IsBlank(dpkMUntil.SelectedDate),
                              Set(varFormErr, "Choose the date the repeats stop."),
                              varFormMode <> "edit" && drpMRepeat.Selected.Value <> "none" && dpkMUntil.SelectedDate < dpkMStart.SelectedDate,
                              Set(varFormErr, "The repeats cannot stop before the first meeting."),
                              varFormMode <> "edit" && drpMRepeat.Selected.Value <> "none" && DateDiff(dpkMStart.SelectedDate, dpkMUntil.SelectedDate, "Days") > 366,
                              Set(varFormErr, "Repeats can run for 12 months at most."),
                              Set(varFormErr, "");
                              If(
                                  varFormMode = "edit",
                                  Patch(
                                      colEvents,
                                      LookUp(colEvents, Id = varEvent.Id),
                                      {
                                      Title: Trim(txtMTitle.Text),
                                      StartDate: dpkMStart.SelectedDate,
                                      EndDate: dpkMEnd.SelectedDate,
                                      TypeKey: drpMType.Selected.TKey,
                                      Status: drpMStatus.Selected.Value,
                                      Programme: drpMProg.Selected.Value,
                                      Kind: drpMKind.Selected.Value,
                                      KeyDate: tglMKey.Value,
                                      BranchReq: tglMBranch.Value,
                                      BranchWho: Trim(txtMWho.Text),
                                      Location: Trim(txtMLoc.Text),
                                      Organiser: Trim(txtMOrg.Text),
                                      Notes: Trim(txtMNotes.Text),
                                      SortKey: DateDiff(Today(), dpkMStart.SelectedDate, "Days") * 1000 - (DateDiff(dpkMStart.SelectedDate, dpkMEnd.SelectedDate, "Days") + 1)
                                      }
                                  );
                                  Set(varEvent, LookUp(colEvents, Id = varEvent.Id)),
                                  If(
                                      drpMRepeat.Selected.Value = "none",
                                      Set(varNewId, Coalesce(Max(colEvents, Id), 0) + 1);
                                      Collect(
                                          colEvents,
                                          {
                                          Id: varNewId,
                                          Sample: false,
                                          Deleted: false,
                                          Overseas: false,
                                          PeopleState: "",
                                          PeopleNote: "",
                                          MoneyState: "",
                                          MoneyNote: "",
                                          EquipState: "",
                                          EquipNote: "",
                                          Title: Trim(txtMTitle.Text),
                                          StartDate: dpkMStart.SelectedDate,
                                          EndDate: dpkMEnd.SelectedDate,
                                          TypeKey: drpMType.Selected.TKey,
                                          Status: drpMStatus.Selected.Value,
                                          Programme: drpMProg.Selected.Value,
                                          Kind: drpMKind.Selected.Value,
                                          KeyDate: tglMKey.Value,
                                          BranchReq: tglMBranch.Value,
                                          BranchWho: Trim(txtMWho.Text),
                                          Location: Trim(txtMLoc.Text),
                                          Organiser: Trim(txtMOrg.Text),
                                          Notes: Trim(txtMNotes.Text),
                                          SortKey: DateDiff(Today(), dpkMStart.SelectedDate, "Days") * 1000 - (DateDiff(dpkMStart.SelectedDate, dpkMEnd.SelectedDate, "Days") + 1)
                                          }
                                      )
                          ,
                                      Set(varNewId, Coalesce(Max(colEvents, Id), 0));
                                      Collect(
                                          colEvents,
                                          ForAll(
                                              Filter(Sequence(60) As qq, If(drpMRepeat.Selected.Value = "weekly", DateAdd(dpkMStart.SelectedDate, (qq.Value - 1) * 7, "Days"), DateAdd(dpkMStart.SelectedDate, qq.Value - 1, "Months")) <= dpkMUntil.SelectedDate) As q,
                                              {
                                              Id: varNewId + q.Value,
                                              Sample: false,
                                              Deleted: false,
                                              Overseas: false,
                                              PeopleState: "",
                                              PeopleNote: "",
                                              MoneyState: "",
                                              MoneyNote: "",
                                              EquipState: "",
                                              EquipNote: "",
                                              Title: Trim(txtMTitle.Text),
                                              StartDate: If(drpMRepeat.Selected.Value = "weekly", DateAdd(dpkMStart.SelectedDate, (q.Value - 1) * 7, "Days"), DateAdd(dpkMStart.SelectedDate, q.Value - 1, "Months")),
                                              EndDate: DateAdd(If(drpMRepeat.Selected.Value = "weekly", DateAdd(dpkMStart.SelectedDate, (q.Value - 1) * 7, "Days"), DateAdd(dpkMStart.SelectedDate, q.Value - 1, "Months")), DateDiff(dpkMStart.SelectedDate, dpkMEnd.SelectedDate, "Days"), "Days"),
                                              TypeKey: drpMType.Selected.TKey,
                                              Status: drpMStatus.Selected.Value,
                                              Programme: drpMProg.Selected.Value,
                                              Kind: drpMKind.Selected.Value,
                                              KeyDate: tglMKey.Value,
                                              BranchReq: tglMBranch.Value,
                                              BranchWho: Trim(txtMWho.Text),
                                              Location: Trim(txtMLoc.Text),
                                              Organiser: Trim(txtMOrg.Text),
                                              Notes: Trim(txtMNotes.Text),
                                              SortKey: DateDiff(Today(), If(drpMRepeat.Selected.Value = "weekly", DateAdd(dpkMStart.SelectedDate, (q.Value - 1) * 7, "Days"), DateAdd(dpkMStart.SelectedDate, q.Value - 1, "Months")), "Days") * 1000 - (DateDiff(dpkMStart.SelectedDate, dpkMEnd.SelectedDate, "Days") + 1)
                                              }
                                          )
                                      )
                                  )
                              );
                              ClearCollect(colScope, Filter(colEvents, !Deleted && (varProg = "all" || (varProg = "national" && Programme = "National Activity") || (varProg = "ctc" && Programme = "CTC") || (varProg = "ctt" && Programme = "CTTs") || (varProg = "overseas" && Overseas) || (varProg = "meet" && Kind <> "event"))));
                              ClearCollect(
                                  colVisible,
                                  AddColumns(
                                      SortByColumns(
                                          Filter(
                                              colScope,
                                              (TypeKey in colSelTypes.TKey) &&
                                              (varStatus = "All" || Status = varStatus) &&
                                              (IsBlank(varSearch) || Lower(varSearch) in Lower(Title))
                                          ),
                                          "SortKey",
                                          SortOrder.Ascending
                                      ),
                                      Concern,
                                      !Deleted && EndDate >= Today() && DateDiff(Today(), StartDate, "Days") <= varThresh && (PeopleState = "needs" || MoneyState = "needs" || EquipState = "needs"),
                                      Hex,
                                      With({k: TypeKey}, LookUp(colTypes, TKey = k).Hex)
                                  )
                              );
                              ClearCollect(
                                  colDays,
                                  ForAll(
                                      Sequence(42) As s,
                                      With(
                                          {d: DateAdd(varGridStart, s.Value - 1, "Days")},
                                          With(
                                              {f: Filter(colVisible, StartDate <= d && EndDate >= d)},
                                              With(
                                                  {e1: If(CountRows(f) >= 1, Index(f, 1)), e2: If(CountRows(f) >= 2, Index(f, 2)), e3: If(CountRows(f) >= 3, Index(f, 3))},
                                                  {
                                                      Idx: s.Value,
                                                      D: d,
                                                      H: Coalesce(LookUp(colBank, BD = d).Name, ""),
                                                      N: CountRows(f),
                                                      T1: If(IsBlank(e1), "", If(e1.Concern, "! ", "") & e1.Title),
                                                      F1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e1.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e1.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e1.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, If(e1.Concern, 2, If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S1: !IsBlank(e1) && e1.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e1.Concern,
                                                      I1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, e1.Id),
                                                      T2: If(IsBlank(e2), "", If(e2.Concern, "! ", "") & e2.Title),
                                                      F2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e2.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e2.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e2.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, If(e2.Concern, 2, If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S2: !IsBlank(e2) && e2.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e2.Concern,
                                                      I2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, e2.Id),
                                                      T3: If(IsBlank(e3), "", If(e3.Concern, "! ", "") & e3.Title),
                                                      F3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e3.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                      B3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e3.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e3.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                      W3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, If(e3.Concern, 2, If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                      S3: !IsBlank(e3) && e3.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e3.Concern,
                                                      I3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, e3.Id)
                                                  }
                                              )
                                          )
                                      )
                                  )
                              );
                              Notify(If(varFormMode = "edit", "Changes saved", "Added") & " (dummy data only: not stored).", NotificationType.Success);
                              Set(varDlg, If(varFormMode = "edit", "detail", ""))
                          )
                        PressedColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        PressedFill: =RGBA(83, 42, 69, 1)
                        RadiusBottomLeft: =17
                        RadiusBottomRight: =17
                        RadiusTopLeft: =17
                        RadiusTopRight: =17
                        Size: =11
                        Text: =If(varFormMode = "edit", "Save changes", "Save")
                        Width: =120
                        X: =Parent.Width - 136

                  - btnDelYes:
                      Control: Classic/Button@2.2.0
                      Properties:

                        FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                        Height: =34
                        HoverColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        HoverFill: =RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1)
                        OnSelect: |-
                          =Patch(colEvents, LookUp(colEvents, Id = varEvent.Id), {Deleted: true});
                          ClearCollect(colScope, Filter(colEvents, !Deleted && (varProg = "all" || (varProg = "national" && Programme = "National Activity") || (varProg = "ctc" && Programme = "CTC") || (varProg = "ctt" && Programme = "CTTs") || (varProg = "overseas" && Overseas) || (varProg = "meet" && Kind <> "event"))));
                          ClearCollect(
                              colVisible,
                              AddColumns(
                                  SortByColumns(
                                      Filter(
                                          colScope,
                                          (TypeKey in colSelTypes.TKey) &&
                                          (varStatus = "All" || Status = varStatus) &&
                                          (IsBlank(varSearch) || Lower(varSearch) in Lower(Title))
                                      ),
                                      "SortKey",
                                      SortOrder.Ascending
                                  ),
                                  Concern,
                                  !Deleted && EndDate >= Today() && DateDiff(Today(), StartDate, "Days") <= varThresh && (PeopleState = "needs" || MoneyState = "needs" || EquipState = "needs"),
                                  Hex,
                                  With({k: TypeKey}, LookUp(colTypes, TKey = k).Hex)
                              )
                          );
                          ClearCollect(
                              colDays,
                              ForAll(
                                  Sequence(42) As s,
                                  With(
                                      {d: DateAdd(varGridStart, s.Value - 1, "Days")},
                                      With(
                                          {f: Filter(colVisible, StartDate <= d && EndDate >= d)},
                                          With(
                                              {e1: If(CountRows(f) >= 1, Index(f, 1)), e2: If(CountRows(f) >= 2, Index(f, 2)), e3: If(CountRows(f) >= 3, Index(f, 3))},
                                              {
                                                  Idx: s.Value,
                                                  D: d,
                                                  H: Coalesce(LookUp(colBank, BD = d).Name, ""),
                                                  N: CountRows(f),
                                                  T1: If(IsBlank(e1), "", If(e1.Concern, "! ", "") & e1.Title),
                                                  F1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e1.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                  B1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e1.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e1.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                  W1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, If(e1.Concern, 2, If(e1.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                  S1: !IsBlank(e1) && e1.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e1.Concern,
                                                  I1: If(IsBlank(e1), 0, e1.Id),
                                                  T2: If(IsBlank(e2), "", If(e2.Concern, "! ", "") & e2.Title),
                                                  F2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e2.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                  B2: If(IsBlank(e2), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e2.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e2.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                  W2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, If(e2.Concern, 2, If(e2.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                  S2: !IsBlank(e2) && e2.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e2.Concern,
                                                  I2: If(IsBlank(e2), 0, e2.Id),
                                                  T3: If(IsBlank(e3), "", If(e3.Concern, "! ", "") & e3.Title),
                                                  F3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e3.Hex), 0.8)),
                                                  B3: If(IsBlank(e3), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), If(e3.Concern, RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1), If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", ColorValue(e3.Hex), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)))),
                                                  W3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, If(e3.Concern, 2, If(e3.Status = "To be confirmed", 1, 0))),
                                                  S3: !IsBlank(e3) && e3.Status = "To be confirmed" && !e3.Concern,
                                                  I3: If(IsBlank(e3), 0, e3.Id)
                                              }
                                          )
                                      )
                                  )
                              )
                          );
                          Notify("Moved to Deleted events (dummy data only).", NotificationType.Success);
                          Set(varDlg, "")
                        PressedColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        PressedFill: =RGBA(142, 21, 55, 1)
                        RadiusBottomLeft: =17
                        RadiusBottomRight: =17
                        RadiusTopLeft: =17
                        RadiusTopRight: =17
                        Size: =11
                        Text: ="Delete event"
                        Width: =122
                        X: =Parent.Width - 142
                        Y: =Parent.Height - 52

                          ClearCollect(colScope, Filter(colEvents, !Deleted && (varProg = "all" || (varProg = "national" && Programme = "National Activity") || (varProg = "ctc" && Programme = "CTC") || (varProg = "ctt" && Programme = "CTTs") || (varProg = "overseas" && Overseas) || (varProg = "meet" && Kind <> "event"))));
                          ClearCollect(
                              colVisible,
                              AddColumns(
                                  SortByColumns(
                                      Filter(
                                          colScope,
                                          (TypeKey in colSelTypes.TKey) &&

                              )

                                      With(

                                          With(

                                              {
                                                  Idx: s.Value,
                                                  D: d,
                                                  H: Coalesce(LookUp(colBank, BD = d).Name, ""),

                                                  F1: If(IsBlank(e1), RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0), ColorFade(ColorValue(e1.Hex), 0.8)),
